#### [4. Collective Ai Tools](/content/Hyraze/collective-ai-tools/README.md)

##### 3D

* [objekto.io](https://objekto.io/en) - Turns a few words, a photo or a sketch into a multi-part, print-ready 3D model designed by AI, then has it 3D printed and delivered (France only for now). `#freemium`

##### Design

* [DESIGN:OS Studio](https://jangtrinh.github.io/design-os-studio/) - FREE desktop design studio where your own AI agent designs on .fig files and you refine on a GPU canvas. `#free` `#design`

##### Developer Tools

* [MailVeri MCP (⭐1)](https://github.com/mailveri/mailveri-mcp) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-01 - Open-source MCP server that lets Claude, Cursor and other agents verify single emails and bulk lists via the MailVeri API (SMTP, catch-all, disposable and role detection). `#freemium` `#opensource` `#mcp`
* [Continua](https://continua-nu.vercel.app/) - Experimental macOS 13+ companion designed to resume interrupted Codex Desktop coding tasks after supported usage resets, with local task history; unsigned/not notarized, manual updates, and genuine unattended recovery unverified. `#free`
* [HopBase](https://hop-base.com/) - AI API gateway with one base URL for Claude, GPT, Gemini, DeepSeek and Qwen text models plus image and video models such as Seedream, Seedance and Kling, compatible with OpenAI, Anthropic and Gemini endpoints. `#paid` `#api` `#llm`

##### Human Resources

* [CookLLM](https://cookllm.com/en/jobs/match) - AI job matcher that emails you new AI jobs from 1,300+ AI companies' career pages and hiring posts on X and LinkedIn, scored against your resume. `#paid`

##### Image Generator

* [AI Anime Generator](https://ai-anime-generator.io/) - Create anime portraits and original characters from text or reference images, with 40 one-time signup credits. `#freemium`
* [ThumbnailSnap](https://thumbnailsnap.com/youtube-thumbnail-maker) - Generates YouTube, Shorts and TikTok thumbnails with AI and keeps the title as an editable text layer; cropping your own photo and adding text is free. `#freemium`

##### Marketing

* [Revenue Scout](https://revenuescout.ai/) - Finds public posts where people need what you sell and drafts replies for you to review and post. `#freemium` `#lead-generation`

##### Meeting

* [Meetini](https://meetini.net/) - Meeting and interview assistant for Apple Silicon Macs with local captions, transcripts, meeting notes, and metered AI assistance. `#paid`

##### Prompts

* [AgentCraft Nano Banana 2.1 Prompts](https://agentcraft.dev/en/prompts/image/nano-banana-2-1/) - 90 Nano Banana 2.1 image prompts, each shown with its output, in a free library of 2,473 image and video prompts that also covers Seedance 2.5 and Kling 4.0. `#free`
* [Prompt Generator](https://trypromptgenerator.com) - Writes prompts in the syntax of the AI tool you pick (ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, Midjourney, Nano Banana, Veo, Sora and more), with 10 free prompts a day and no sign-up. `#freemium` `#writing`

#### [5. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Commands & Extensions

* [Kraft Lite (⭐13)](https://github.com/itsOmidKarami/kraft/tree/main/plugins/kraft-lite) ⭐ 13 | 🐛 40 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-12 - Gemini CLI extension (also a Claude Code plugin) that walks a Kraft chain from spec to pull request inside one session: steps run in order from the chain file, it stops at each gate for a person's answer, and it caps its fix loops. No server; install from a clone with `gemini extensions install <clone>/plugins/kraft-lite`. Apache-2.0.
* [ClipUGC (⭐4)](https://github.com/clipugc/ClipUGC-CLI) ⭐ 5 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-12 - Gemini CLI extension that makes UGC style vertical videos for your app with AI influencers who keep the same face: describe an AI influencer, add a screen recording of your app, and get a ready-to-post video with a hook line for TikTok, Reels and Shorts. Bundles the hosted ClipUGC MCP server (OAuth sign-in, no API key), four Agent Skills and a `GEMINI.md` context file; needs a ClipUGC account, with free credits to try. Local stdio alternative: `npx -y clipugc mcp` with `CLIPUGC_API_KEY` (paid plans). MIT. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/clipugc/ClipUGC-CLI`.

#### [9. Free for Dev](/content/ripienaar/free-for-dev/README.md)

##### Messaging and Streaming

* [GravEx Chat](https://chat.gravex.app/en/) - Free chat sdk that works for any client, like web, Android, iOS or whatever. Create API key and hand it over to your own Agent(Claude, Codex...) with [llms.txt](https://chat.gravex.app/en/llms.txt), then you get your messenger to your service. It's that simple.

##### Web Hosting

* [Reliant](https://reliantlabs.io) - Host, monitor, and deploy your services with production best practices. Tracing, continuous profiling, metrics, and logs fully baked in and correlated with each release allow you to identify and auto rollback on demand. One free static site per organization, 1 GiB of storage, 10 GiB of traffic a month, CDN with HTTPS, custom domains, no card needed, deployed with the open-source forge CLI.

##### Analytics, Events and Statistics

* [RudderStack](https://rudderstack.com/) - Event streaming to 200+ destinations and data warehouse, plus reverse ETL. Comes with MCP server and CLI for AI agents. Free forever for 250k events/month.

#### [10. Awesome Research](/content/emptymalei/awesome-research/README.md)

##### Plotting and Charting Online / Enterprise Git Service

* [Scientific Figure](https://scientificfigure.com/): AI tool that draws scientific illustrations, graphical abstracts, data plots, and conference posters from a text prompt, sketch, or PDF; exports SVG and PPTX at journal column widths.

##### Datasets / Social Sciences

* [twtData X Trends Archive](https://twtdata.com/twitter-trends/) (`Lookup`): X (Twitter) trending topics by country and city, with a page for each past day; the largest countries are recorded hourly.

##### Writing / Social Sciences

* [Articlo](https://getarticlo.com/): Prepares existing Word manuscripts for a target journal with an editable document, change summary, and author checklist. Free readiness check; paid manuscript preparation.

##### Notebooks for Researchers / Social Sciences

* [Glasp](https://glasp.co): Web and YouTube highlighter with AI summaries and notes, plus Kindle highlights import. Browser extensions, iOS and Android apps, and an MCP server; export to Notion, Obsidian and Zotero.

##### Editors / Markdown

* [markon](https://metaory.github.io/markon/) (`Cloud`, `Local PWA`): Minimal distraction-free live Markdown editor.

##### Editors / LaTeX

* [Manulyx](https://manulyx.com/)(`Cloud`): Online LaTeX editor for writing and revising papers with co-authors: real-time co-editing, track changes, publisher templates, submission stages, response-to-reviewers letters and marked manuscripts. Free plan available.
* [Oleafly](https://oleafly.com) - Open-source desktop editor for LaTeX, Typst and Markdown with a built-in LaTeX engine, live PDF preview and Zotero citations. Runs offline on macOS, Windows and Linux.

##### Code Editors / HTML+CSS+JS

* [neovim](https://neovim.io/)(`Free`,`Cross-platform`,`Plugins`): Vim-fork focused on extensibility and usability.

##### Investigate Papers / HTML+CSS+JS

* [JournalCompass](https://journalcompass.com): Free bilingual journal finder: CAS zones, impact factors, review times, APC/OA status and an AI journal matcher.

##### Bibliography / HTML+CSS+JS

* [citeguard (⭐1)](https://github.com/wedo911/citeguard) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-26: Checks a `.bib`/`.ris` bibliography (or a single DOI) for cited papers that have been retracted, corrected, or flagged with an expression of concern, via Crossref and the Retraction Watch records it carries. Python library/CLI, MCP server, and GitHub Action; no API key. `pip install citeguard-cli`
* [Reference Verifier (⭐0)](https://github.com/the-aurora-lab/reference-verifier) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-02: Checks that every reference in a .bib file or LaTeX paper exists in Crossref, OpenAlex, DBLP, arXiv or PubMed, and flags invented papers and wrong DOIs. Open source (MIT).

##### Tips for Researchers / HTML+CSS+JS

* [ProfScout](https://profscout.com): Finds professors most likely to reply to research cold emails, ranked by reply-fit. Freemium.

##### More / HTML+CSS+JS

* [ToolStack](https://tools.mindutil.com/en): a free toolbox of 122 browser-based utilities (text, JSON, image, PDF, unit conversion, color) that run entirely client-side, with no signup and no file uploads.

* [Firecrawl (⭐200k)](https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl) ⭐ 201,068 | 🐛 794 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-11-12 - Open-source web data API that scrapes, crawls, maps, and searches the web and returns Markdown or structured JSON for data pipelines and LLM ingestion.
* [OLake (⭐1.6k)](https://github.com/datazip-inc/olake) ⭐ 1,581 | 🐛 194 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-11-12 - Open-source replication of Postgres, MySQL, MongoDB, Oracle, SQL Server, DB2, Kafka and S3 into Apache Iceberg, with CDC and table maintenance. Written in Go.
* [Renart (⭐96)](https://github.com/renart-data/renart) ⭐ 96 | 🐛 43 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-11-12 - Local-first IDE for authoring and running SQL and Python data pipelines stored in Git.
* [mq-bridge (⭐25)](https://github.com/marcomq/mq-bridge) ⭐ 27 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-11-11 - Embeddable Rust engine and CLI that moves data between Kafka, NATS, RabbitMQ, MQTT, SQL databases, Postgres/MongoDB CDC and Parquet on S3/GCS/Azure, with bindings for Python and Node.js.
* [adbcBridge (⭐2)](https://github.com/singhpratech/adbcbridge) ⭐ 3 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 C | 📅 2026-11-12 - Driver for ADBC, Apache Arrow's database connectivity API, that sits on top of any ODBC driver, so databases with no native Arrow driver return columnar Arrow record batches and take bulk ingest.

##### Serialization format

* [SequenceFile](https://cwiki.apache.org/confluence/display/HADOOP2/SequenceFile) - A flat file consisting of binary key/value pairs. It is extensively used in MapReduce as input/output formats.

##### Stream Processing

* [Timeplus Proton (⭐2.3k)](https://github.com/timeplus-io/proton) ⭐ 2,281 | 🐛 75 | 🌐 C++ | 📅 2026-11-10 - Streaming SQL engine in a single C++ binary, powered by ClickHouse; a lightweight alternative to Flink and ksqlDB for streaming ETL, windowed aggregations, joins and materialized views over Kafka.
* [CardinalityEstimation (⭐100)](https://github.com/saguiitay/CardinalityEstimation) ⭐ 100 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 C# | 📅 2026-11-06 - C# library using HyperLogLog for approximate distinct counting in data streams, with mergeable estimators and serialization.
* [Spring Cloud Dataflow](https://dataflow.spring.io/) - Streaming and tasks execution between Spring Boot apps.

##### Charts and Dashboards

* [SmoothieCharts (⭐2.3k)](https://github.com/joewalnes/smoothie) ⭐ 2,276 | 🐛 28 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2023-01-08 - A JavaScript Charting Library for Streaming Data.
* [GeoSQL (⭐684)](https://github.com/dekart-xyz/geosql) ⭐ 685 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-09 - Claude/Codex skill for cost-safe geospatial SQL on BigQuery and Snowflake.
* [Streamlit](https://streamlit.io/) - An open-source Python framework for building interactive data apps and dashboards with minimal code. Connects to any data source and supports real-time updates, caching, and one-click deployment.

##### Workflow

* [Dataform (⭐1k)](https://github.com/dataform-co/dataform) ⭐ 1,000 | 🐛 112 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-11-12 - An open-source framework and web based IDE to manage datasets and their dependencies. SQLX extends your existing SQL warehouse dialect to add features that support dependency management, testing, documentation and more.
* [Ciaren (⭐37)](https://github.com/ciaren-labs/Ciaren) ⭐ 37 | 🐛 11 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-12 - A local-first visual ETL and ML workflow builder with per-step previews, cron scheduling, and export to pandas or Polars code.
* [Cascading](https://cascading.wensel.net/) - Java based application development platform.

* [dataprof (⭐33)](https://github.com/AndreaBozzo/dataprof) ⭐ 34 | 🐛 106 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-11-11 - Data profiling and quality gates for CSV, JSON, Parquet, DataFrames and Arrow tables, with a Rust core and a Python API.

##### Tools

* [Terrazzo](https://terrazzo.app/) - Generate code for web, mobile and native apps from design tokens JSON in the Design Tokens Community Group format.

##### Develop isolated components / Storybook addons

* [Accessibility](https://storybook.js.org/addons/@storybook/addon-a11y) - Test component compliance with web accessibility standards.
* [Actions](https://storybook.js.org/addons/@storybook/addon-actions) - Get UI feedback when an action is performed on an interactive element.
* [Backgrounds](https://storybook.js.org/addons/@storybook/addon-backgrounds) - Switch backgrounds to view components in different settings.
* [Docs](https://storybook.js.org/addons/@storybook/addon-docs) - Document component usage and properties in Markdown.
* [Links](https://storybook.js.org/addons/@storybook/addon-links) - Link stories together to build demos and prototypes with your UI components.
* [Source](https://storybook.js.org/addons/@storybook/addon-storysource) - View a story's source code to see how it works and paste into your app.
* [Viewport](https://storybook.js.org/addons/@storybook/addon-viewport) - Build responsive components by adjusting Storybook's viewport size and orientation.
* [Vitest](https://storybook.js.org/addons/@storybook/addon-vitest) - Run every story as a Vitest test, with interaction, accessibility and visual checks.

##### Component patterns / Storybook addons

* [ARIA Authoring Practices Guide patterns](https://www.w3.org/WAI/ARIA/apg/patterns/) - Reference patterns from the W3C for building accessible components, with keyboard interaction and working examples.
* [Open UI](https://open-ui.org/) - W3C Community Group that researches how design systems name and build components, and proposes new native HTML controls.
* [The Component Gallery](https://component.gallery/) - Up-to-date repository of interface components based on examples from real design systems.

##### Headless component libraries / Storybook addons

* [Ark UI](https://ark-ui.com/) - Unstyled, accessible components for your design system that work in React, Vue, Solid and Svelte.
* [Base UI](https://base-ui.com/) - Unstyled components for building accessible design systems, from the creators of Radix, Floating UI and Material UI.
* [Radix Primitives](https://www.radix-ui.com/primitives) - Open-source component library for building high-quality, accessible design systems and web apps.
* [React Aria](https://react-aria.adobe.com/) - Accessible components and hooks with behavior, internationalization and no imposed styles, by Adobe.
* [shadcn/ui](https://ui.shadcn.com/) - Composable, accessible components you copy into your codebase and own, distributed through a registry.

##### Pattern libraries articles / Storybook addons

* [Design systems workflow in Storybook](https://www.chromatic.com/blog/design-systems-in-storybook/)
* [How packaging makes it dead simple to share UI components](https://www.chromatic.com/blog/how-packaging-makes-it-dead-simple-to-share-ui-components/)

##### AI and design systems / Storybook addons

* [DESIGN.md (⭐29k)](https://github.com/google-labs-code/design.md) ⭐ 28,831 | 🐛 40 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-11-06 - Format specification for describing a visual identity and design system to coding agents.
* [Figma Console MCP (⭐3.3k)](https://github.com/southleft/figma-console-mcp) ⭐ 3,287 | 🐛 16 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-29 - MCP server that connects AI assistants to Figma for design system extraction, creation and debugging.
* [Figma Code Connect (⭐1.6k)](https://github.com/figma/code-connect) ⭐ 1,606 | 🐛 199 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-26 - Connects design system components in code with their counterparts in Figma, so developers and agents get real snippets.
* [Figma MCP server](https://developers.figma.com/docs/figma-mcp-server/) - Official server that gives AI agents the design context of your Figma files to generate code, and lets them write back to the canvas.
* [Storybook MCP addon](https://storybook.js.org/addons/@storybook/addon-mcp) - Helps agents write and test stories for your UI components.

##### Unit & Regression test / Storybook addons

* [Chromatic](https://www.chromatic.com/) - Visual testing for React, Angular and Vue. Chromatic ensures consistency in UI components, down to the pixel. Every commit is automatically tested for visual changes in the cloud.

##### Books / Storybook addons

* [Building Design Systems](https://link.springer.com/book/10.1007/978-1-4842-4514-9) - Unify user experiences through a shared design language by Sarrah Vesselov and Taurie Davis.
* [Frontend Architecture for Design Systems](https://www.oreilly.com/library/view/frontend-architecture-for/9781491926772/) - A modern blueprint for scalable and sustainable websites by Micah Godbolt.

##### Talks / Storybook addons
